============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_documents_worker(docs_batch: list[Document]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to tokenize a batch of documents.\n\n    This function is executed in a separate process and must be picklable.\n\n    Args:\n        docs_batch: List of Document objects to process\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping doc_id -> (term_set, term_frequencies)\n    \"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    for doc in docs_batch:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        result[doc.doc_id] = (set(tf.keys()), tf)\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_inverted_index_for_terms_worker(args: tuple) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to build inverted index for a batch of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_batch, all_doc_term_freqs, idf_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping term -> sorted posting list\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_batch, all_doc_term_freqs, idf_dict = args\n    result = {}\n\n    for term in terms_batch:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in all_doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        result[term] = posting_list\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_worker(args: tuple) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to build document vectors for a batch of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids_batch, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping doc_id ->        result[doc_id] = (doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared))\n\n    return result\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _chunks(items: list, chunk_size: int) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Divide items into chunks of specified size.\"\"\"\n    chunks_list = []\n    for i in range(0, len(items), chunk_size):\n        chunks_list.append(items[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks_list\n\n\ndef _merge_dicts_of_dicts(dict_list: list[dict]) -> ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building - Two-Phase Approach\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Uses a two-phase approach:\n    Phase 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation on document batches\n    Phase 2: Sequential document frequency calculation, then parallel inverted\n             index and document vector construction\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for Phase 1\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel Tokenization and TF Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    doc_chunks = _chunks(documents, chunk_size)\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        chunk_results = list(executor.map(_tokenize_documents_worker, doc_chunks))\n\n    # Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n\n    for chunk_result in chunk_results:\n        for doc_id, (term_set, tf_dict) in chunk_result.items():\n            doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf_dict\n            doc_terms[doc_id] = term_set\n            index.vocabulary.update(term_set)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Sequential Document Frequency Calculation\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Compute IDF Scores (Sequential, minimal computation)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Parallel Inverted Index Building (by terms)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunks = _chunks(vocab_list, chunk_size)\n\n    # Prepare arguments for workers\n    worker_args = [\n        (vocab_chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks\n    ]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        index_parts = list(executor.map(_build_inverted_index_for_terms_worker, worker_args))\n\n    # Merge inverted index parts\n    for index_part in index_parts:\n        index.inverted_index.update(index_part)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Parallel Document Vector Construction\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    doc_ids_list = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunks = _chunks(doc_ids_list, chunk_size)\n\n    # Prepare arguments for workers\n    worker_args = [\n        (doc_chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for doc_chunk in doc_chunks\n    ]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n in doc_vector_part.items():\n            index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n            index.doc_norms[doc_id] = norm\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_worker(args: tuple) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to perform a single query search.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, documents_dict = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = documents_dict.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results list, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # all_results = list(executor.map(_search_worker, worker_args))\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return (all_results, elapsed)\n\n\n# {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Build index\n    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index to their normalized frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n\n    total = len(tokens)\n ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_documents_worker(docs_batch: list[Document]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to tokenize a Dictionary mapping doc_id -> (term_set, term_frequencies)\n    \"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    for doc in docs_batch:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        result[doc.doc_id] = (set(tf.keys()), tf)\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_inverted_index_for_terms_worker(args: tuple) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _chunks(items: list, chunk_size: int) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Divide items into chunks of specified size.\"\"\"\n    chunks_list = []\n    for i in range(0, len(items), chunk_size):\n        chunks_list.append(items[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks_list\n\n\ndef _merge_dicts_of_dicts(dict_list: list[dict]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Merge a list of dictionaries into a single dictionary.\"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    for d in dict_list:\n        result.update(d)\n    return result\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building - Two-Phase Approach\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Uses a two-phase approach:\n    Phase 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation on document batches\n    Phase 2: Sequential document frequency calculation, then parallel inverted\n             index and document vector construction\n\n    Optimized to minimize worker startup overhead by using fewer workers and\n    larger chunks for Phase 1 (which is the bottleneck).\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: 2-4)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for Phase 1\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        # Reduce worker count to minimize startup overhead\n        # Tokenization is the main bottleneck, balance with startup cost\n        num_workers = max(2, cpu_count() // 2)\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel Tokenization and TF Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    # This is the main CPU-bound work. Use larger chunks to reduce overhead.\n\n    # Ensure chunk size is large enough to justify worker startup\n    effective_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(documents) // (num_workers * 2))\n    doc_chunks = _chunks(documents, effective_chunk_size)\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_tokenize_documents_worker, doc_chunks)\n\n    # Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n\n    for chunk_result in chunk_results:\n        for doc_id, (term_set, tf_dict) in chunk_result.items():\n            doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf_dict\n            doc_terms[doc_id] = term_set\n            index.vocabulary.update(term_set)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Sequential Document Frequency Calculation\n    # ========================================================================\n    # This is sequential - no benefit to parallelization\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Compute IDF Scores (Sequential, minimal computation)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Parallel Inverted Index and Document Vector Building\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Combine these into one worker pool to amortize startup cost\n\n    # Split into 2 tasks: one for inverted index, one for doc vectors\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    doc_ids_list = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n\n    worker_args = [\n        (\"inverted_index\", vocab_list, doc_term_freqs, index.idf),\n        (\"doc_vectors\", doc_ids_list, doc_term_freqs, index.idf),\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=2) as pool:\n        phase2_results = pool.map(_phase2_builder, worker_args)\n\n    # Extract results\n    for result_type, result_data in phase2_results:\n        if result_type == \"inverted_index\":\n            index.inverted_index.update(result_data)\n        elif result_type == \"doc_vectors\":\n            for doc_id, (doc_vector, norm) in result_data.items():\n                index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n                index.doc_norms[doc_id] = norm\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_worker(args: tuple) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to perform a single query search.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, documents_dict = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = documents_dict.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results list, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Execute parallel search\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        all_results = list(executor.map(_search_worker, worker_args))\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return (all_results, elapsed)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=None, help=\"Number of workers\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--chunk-size\", type=int, default=500, help=\"Chunk size for parallel processing\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Build index\n    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index (parallel)...\")\n    result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n        documents,\n        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n        chunk_size=args.chunk_size,\n    )\n\n    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n\n    # Perform search\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for: '{args.query}'\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\n    all_results, search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        [args.query],\n        result.index,\n        args.top_k,\n        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n        documents=documents,\n    )\n\n    results = all_results[0] if all_results else []\n\n    print(f\"Search completed in {search_time*1000:.2f} ms\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop {len(results)} results:\")\n    for i, res in enumerate(results, 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}. [{res.score:.4f}] {res.title} (doc_id: {res.doc_id})\")\n\n    return result\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":292,"oldLines":6,"newStart":292,"newLines":51,"lines":["     return result"," "," ","+def _phase2_builder(args: tuple) -> tuple:","+    \"\"\"","+    Worker function to build either inverted index or document vectors.","+    Reduces number of worker pool creations for better overhead amortization.","+","+    Args:","+        args: Tuple of (task_type, items, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict)","+","+    Returns:","+        Tuple of (task_type, result_dict)","+    \"\"\"","+    task_type, items, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict = args","+","+    if task_type == \"inverted_index\":","+        # \"may\",\n        \"been\",\n        \"being\",\n        \"am\",\n    ]\n)\n\nTOKEN_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{2,}\\b\")\n\n\ndef tokenize(text: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Tokenize TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in STOP_WORDS]\n\n\ndef compute_term_frequencies(tokens: list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute normalized term frequencies for a list of tokens.\n\n    TF(t, d) = count(t in d) / total_terms(d)\n\n    Args:\n        tokens: List of tokens from a document\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping terms to their normalized frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n\n    total = len(tokens)\n ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TFIDFIndex:\n    \"\"\"\n    TF-IDF inverted index for similarity search.\n\n    Attributes:\n \"\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_documents_worker(docs_batch: list[Document]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to tokenize a batch of documents.\n\n    This function is executed in a separate process and must be picklable.\n\n    Args:\n        docs_batch: List of Document objects to process\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping doc_id -> (term_set, term_frequencies)\n    \"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    for doc in docs_batch:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n Worker function to build inverted index for a batch of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_batch, all_doc_term_freqs, idf_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping term -> sorted posting list\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_batch, all_doc_term_freqs, idf_dict = args\n    result = {}\n\n    for term in terms_batch:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in       posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        result[term] = posting_list\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_worker(args: tuple) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        result[doc_id] = (doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared))\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef _phase2_builder(args: tuple) -> tuple:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to build either inverted index or document vectors.\n    Reduces number of worker pool creations for better overhead amortization.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (task_type, items, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (task_type, result_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    task_type, items, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict = args\n\n    if task_type == \"inverted_index\":\n        # Build inverted index for all terms\n        result = {}\n        for term in items:\n            posting_list = []\n            for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n                if term in tf_dict:\n                    tf = tf_dict[term]\n                    tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                    posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n            posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            result[term] = posting_list\n        return (task_type, result)\n\n    elif task_type == \"doc_vectors\":\n        # Build document vectors for all documents\n        result = {}\n        for doc_id in items:\n            tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            result[doc_id] = (doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared))\n        return (task_type, result)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _chunks(items: list, chunk_size: int) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Divide items into chunks of specified size.\"\"\"\n    chunks_list = []\n    for i in range(0, len(items), chunk_size):\n        chunks_list.append(items[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks_list\n\n\ndef _merge_dicts_of_dicts(dict_list: list[dict]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Merge a list of dictionaries into a single dictionary.\"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    for d in dict_list:\n        result.update(d)\n    return result\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building - Two-Phase Approach\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Uses a two-phase approach:\n    Phase 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation on document batches\n    Phase 2: Sequential document frequency calculation, then parallel inverted\n             index and document vector construction\n\n    Optimized to minimize worker startup overhead by using fewer workers and\n    larger chunks for Phase 1 (which is the bottleneck).\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: 2-4)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for Phase 1\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        # Reduce worker count to minimize startup overhead\n        # Tokenization is the main bottleneck, balance with startup cost\n        num_workers = max(2, cpu_count() // 2)\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel Tokenization and TF Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    # This is the main CPU-bound work. Use larger chunks to reduce overhead.\n\n    # Ensure chunk size is large enough to justify worker startup\n    effective_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(documents) // (num_workers * 2))\n    doc_chunks = _chunks(documents, effective_chunk_size)\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_tokenize_documents_worker, doc_chunks)\n\n    # Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n\n    for chunk_result in chunk_results:\n        for doc_id, (term_set, tf_dict) in chunk_result.items():\n            doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf_dict\n            doc_terms[doc_id] = term_set\n            index.vocabulary.update(term_set)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Sequential Document Frequency Calculation\n    # ========================================================================\n    # This is sequential - no benefit to parallelization\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Compute IDF Scores (Sequential, minimal computation)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Parallel Inverted Index and Document Vector Building\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Combine these into one worker pool to amortize startup cost\n\n    # Split into 2 tasks: one for inverted index, one for doc vectors\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    doc_ids_list = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n\n    worker_args = [\n        (\"inverted_index\", vocab_list, doc_term_freqs, index.idf),\n        (\"doc_vectors\", doc_ids_list, doc_term_freqs, index.idf),\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=2) as pool:\n        phase2_results = pool.map(_phase2_builder, worker_args)\n\n    # Extract results\n    for result_type, result_data in phase2_results:\n        if result_type == \"inverted_index\":\n            index.inverted_index.update(result_data)\n        elif result_type == \"doc_vectors\":\n            for doc_id, (doc_vector, norm) in result_data.items():\n                index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n                index.doc_norms[doc_id] = norm\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_worker(args: tuple) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to perform a single query search.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, documents_dict = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = documents_dict.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results list, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Create document title lookup dictionary\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        for doc in documents:\n            doc_titles[doc.doc_id] = doc.title\n\n    # Prepare arguments for workers\n    worker_args = [\n        (query, index, top_k, doc_titles) for query in queries\n    ]\n\n    # to their normalized frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n\n    total = len(tokens)\n        result[doc_id] = (doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared))\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef _phase2_builder(args: tuple) -> tuple:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to build either inverted index or document vectors.\n    Reduces number of worker pool creations for better overhead amortization.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (task_type, items, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (task_type, result_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    task_type, items, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict = args\n\n    if task_type == \"inverted_index\":\n        # Build inverted index for all terms\n tf_dict[term]\n                    tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                    posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n            posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            result[term] = posting_list\n        return (task_type, result)\n\n    elif task_type == \"doc_vectors\":\n        # Build document vectors for all documents\n        result = {}\n        for doc_id in items:\n            tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            result[doc_id] = (doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared))\n        return (task_type, result)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _chunks(items: list, chunk_size: int) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Divide items into chunks of specified size.\"\"\"\n    chunks_list = []\n    for i in range(0, len(items), chunk_size):\n        chunks_list.append(items[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks_list\n\n\ndef _merge_dicts_of_dicts(dict_list: list[dict]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Merge a list of dictionaries into a single dictionary.\"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    for d in dict_list:\n        result.update(d)\n    return result\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building - Two-Phase Approach\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Uses a two-phase approach:\n    Phase 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation on document batches\n    Phase 2: Sequential document frequency calculation, then parallel inverted\n             index and document vector construction\n\n    Optimized to minimize worker startup overhead by using fewer workers and\n    larger chunks for Phase 1 (which is the bottleneck).\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: 2-4)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for Phase 1\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        # Reduce worker count to minimize startup overhead\n        # Tokenization is the main bottleneck, balance with startup cost\n        num_workers = max(2, cpu_count() // 2)\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel Tokenization and TF Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    # This is the main CPU-bound work. Use larger chunks to reduce overhead.\n\n    # Ensure chunk size is large enough to justify worker startup\n    effective_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(documents) // (num_workers * 2))\n    doc_chunks = _chunks(documents, effective_chunk_size)\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_tokenize_documents_worker, doc_chunks)\n\n    # Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n\n    for chunk_result in chunk_results:\n        for doc_id, (term_set, tf_dict) in chunk_result.items():\n            doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf_dict\n            doc_terms[doc_id] = term_set\n            index.vocabulary.update(term_set)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Sequential Document Frequency Calculation\n    # ========================================================================\n    # This is sequential - no benefit to parallelization\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Compute IDF Scores (Sequential, minimal computation)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Parallel Inverted Index and Document Vector Building\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Combine these into one worker pool to amortize startup cost\n\n    # Split into 2 tasks: one for inverted index, one for doc vectors\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    doc_ids_list = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n\n    worker_args = [\n        (\"inverted_index\", vocab_list, doc_term_freqs, index.idf),\n        (\"doc_vectors\", doc_ids_list, doc_term_freqs, index.idf),\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=2) as pool:\n        phase2_results = pool.map(_phase2_builder, worker_args)\n\n    # Extract results\n    for result_type, result_data in phase2_results:\n        if result_type == \"inverted_index\":\n            index.inverted_index.update(result_data)\n        elif result_type == \"doc_vectors\":\n            for doc_id, (doc_vector, norm) in result_data.items():\n                index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n                index.doc_norms[doc_id] = norm\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_worker(args: tuple) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to perform a single query search.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, documents_dict = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = documents_dict.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _batch_search_worker(args: tuple) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to process a batch of queries.\n\n    Batching multiple queries per worker amortizes worker startup cost.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (queries_batch, index, top_k, documents_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists, one per query\n    \"\"\"\n    queries_batch, index, top_k, documents_dict = args\n    all_results = []\n\n    for query in queries_batch:\n        result = _search_worker((query, index, top_k, documents_dict))\n        all_results.append(result)\n\n    return all_results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results list, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Create document title lookup dictionary\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        for doc in documents:\n            doc_titles[doc.doc_id] = doc.title\n\n    # Prepare arguments for workers\n    worker_args = [\n        (query, index, top_k, doc_titles) for query in queries\n    ]\n\n    # Execute parallel search\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        all_results = list(executor.map(_search_worker, worker_args))\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return (all_results, elapsed)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=None, help=\"Number of workers\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--chunk-size\", type=int, default=500, help=\"Chunk size for parallel processing\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # text into lowercase words, filtering stop words.\n\n    Args:\n        text: Input text string\n\n    Returns:\n        List of lowercase tokens (words)\n    \"\"\"\n    tokens = TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in STOP_WORDS]\n\n\ndef compute_term_frequencies(tokens: list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute normalized term frequencies for a list of tokens.\n\n    TF(t, d) = count(t in d) / total_terms(d)\n\n    Args:\n        tokens: List of tokens from a document\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping terms to their normalized frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TF-IDF Index Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TFIDFIndex:\n    \"\"\"\n    TF-IDF inverted index for similarity search.\n\n    Attributes:\n        num_documents: Total number of documents indexed\n        vocabulary: Set of all unique terms\n        document_frequencies: term -> number of documents containing term\n        idf: term -> inverse document frequency score\n        inverted_index: term -> list of (doc_id, tf-idf score)\n        doc_vectors: doc_id -> dict of term -> tf-idf score\n        doc_norms: doc_id -> L2 norm of document vector\n    \"\"\"\n\n    num_documents: int = 0\n    vocabulary: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)\n    document_frequencies: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    idf: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    inverted_index: dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_vectors: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_norms: dict[int, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SearchResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from similarity search.\"\"\"\n\n    doc_id: int\n    score: float\n    title: str = \"\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Phase 1: Parallel Document Tokenization\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_documents_worker(docs_batch: list[Document]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to tokenize a batch of documents.\n\n    This function is executed in a separate process and must be picklable.\n\n    Args:\n        docs_batch: List of Document objects to process\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping doc_id -> (term_set, term_frequencies)\n    \"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    for doc in docs_batch:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        result[doc.doc_id] = (set(tf.keys()), tf)\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_inverted_index_for_terms_worker(args: tuple) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to build inverted index for a batch of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_batch, all_doc_term_freqs, idf_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping term -> sorted posting list\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_batch, all_doc_term_freqs, idf_dict = args\n    result = {}\n\n    for term in terms_batch:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in all_doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        result[term] = posting_list\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_worker(args: tuple) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to build document vectors for a batch of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids_batch, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping doc_id -> {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        result[doc_id] = (doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared))\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef _phase2_builder(args: tuple) -> tuple:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to build either inverted index or document vectors.\n    Reduces number of worker pool creations for better overhead amortization.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (task_type, items, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (task_type, result_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    task_type, items, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict = args\n\n    if task_type == \"inverted_index\":\n        # Build inverted index for all terms\n        result = {}\n        for term in items:\n            posting_list = []\n            for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n                if term in tf_dict:\n                    tf = tf_dict[term]\n                    tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                    posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n            posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            result[term] = posting_list\n        return (task_type, result)\n\n    elif task_type == \"doc_vectors\":\n        # Build document vectors for all documents\n        result = {}\n        for doc_id in items:\n            tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            result[doc_id] = (doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared))\n        return (task_type, result)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _chunks(items: list, chunk_size: int) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Divide items into chunks of specified size.\"\"\"\n    chunks_list = []\n    for i in range(0, len(items), chunk_size):\n        chunks_list.append(items[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks_list\n\n\ndef _merge_dicts_of_dicts(dict_list: list[dict]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Merge a list of dictionaries into a single dictionary.\"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    for d in dict_list:\n        result.update(d)\n    return result\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building - Two-Phase Approach\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Uses a two-phase approach:\n    Phase 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation on document batches\n    Phase 2: Sequential document frequency calculation, then parallel inverted\n             index and document vector construction\n\n    Optimized to minimize worker startup overhead by using fewer workers and\n    larger chunks for Phase 1 (which is the bottleneck).\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: 2-4)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for Phase 1\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        # Reduce worker count to minimize startup overhead\n        # Tokenization is the main bottleneck, balance with startup cost\n        num_workers = max(2, cpu_count() // 2)\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel Tokenization and TF Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    # This is the main CPU-bound work. Use larger chunks to reduce overhead.\n\n    # Ensure chunk size is large enough to justify worker startup\n    effective_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(documents) // (num_workers * 2))\n    doc_chunks = _chunks(documents, effective_chunk_size)\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_tokenize_documents_worker, doc_chunks)\n\n    # Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n\n    for chunk_result in chunk_results:\n        for doc_id, (term_set, tf_dict) in chunk_result.items():\n            doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf_dict\n            doc_terms[doc_id] = term_set\n            index.vocabulary.update(term_set)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Sequential Document Frequency Calculation\n    # ========================================================================\n    # This is sequential - no benefit to parallelization\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Compute IDF Scores (Sequential, minimal computation)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Parallel Inverted Index and Document Vector Building\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Combine these into one worker pool to amortize startup cost\n\n    # Split into 2 tasks: one for inverted index, one for doc vectors\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    doc_ids_list = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n\n    worker_args = [\n        (\"inverted_index\", vocab_list, doc_term_freqs, index.idf),\n        (\"doc_vectors\", doc_ids_list, doc_term_freqs, index.idf),\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=2) as pool:\n        phase2_results = pool.map(_phase2_builder, worker_args)\n\n    # Extract results\n    for result_type, result_data in phase2_results:\n        if result_type == \"inverted_index\":\n            index.inverted_index.update(result_data)\n        elif result_type == \"doc_vectors\":\n            for doc_id, (doc_vector, norm) in result_data.items():\n                index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n                index.doc_norms[doc_id] = norm\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_worker(args: tuple) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to perform a single query search.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, documents_dict = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = documents_dict.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _batch_search_worker(args: tuple) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to process a batch of queries.\n\n    Batching multiple queries per worker amortizes worker startup cost.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (queries_batch, index, top_k, documents_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists, one per query\n    \"\"\"\n    queries_batch, index, top_k, documents_dict = args\n    all_results = []\n\n    for query in queries_batch:\n        result = _search_worker((query, index, top_k, documents_dict))\n        all_results.append(result)\n\n    return all_results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using batched processing.\n\n    Batches queries together to amortize worker process startup cost.\n    This is critical for good performance with moderate query counts.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: 4)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results list, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    if not queries:\n        return ([], 0.0)\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = 4  # to their normalized frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TF-IDF Index Data Structures\n# \"\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_documents_worker(docs_batch: list[Document]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to tokenize a Worker function to build inverted index for a batch of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_batch, all_doc_term_freqs, idf_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping term -> sorted posting list\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_batch, all_doc_term_freqs, idf_dict = args\n    result = {}\n\n    for term in terms_batch:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in       posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        result[term] = posting_list\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_worker(args: tuple) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        result[doc_id] = (doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared))\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef _phase2_builder(args: tuple) -> tuple:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to build either inverted index or document vectors.\n    Reduces number of worker pool creations for better overhead amortization.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (task_type, items, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (task_type, result_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    task_type, items, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict = args\n\n    if task_type == \"inverted_index\":\n        # Build inverted index for all terms\n tf_dict[term]\n                    tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                    posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n            posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            result[term] = posting_list\n        return (task_type, result)\n\n    elif task_type == \"doc_vectors\":\n        # Build document vectors for all documents\n        result = {}\n        for doc_id in items:\n            tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            result[doc_id] = (doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared))\n        return (task_type, result)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _chunks(items: list, chunk_size: int) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Divide items into chunks of specified size.\"\"\"\n    chunks_list = []\n    for i in range(0, len(items), chunk_size):\n        chunks_list.append(items[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks_list\n\n\ndef _merge_dicts_of_dicts(dict_list: list[dict]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Merge a list of dictionaries into a single dictionary.\"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    for d in dict_list:\n        result.update(d)\n    return result\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building - Two-Phase Approach\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: int = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Uses a two-phase approach:\n    Phase 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation on document batches\n    Phase 2: Sequential document frequency calculation, then parallel inverted\n             index and document vector construction\n\n    Optimized to minimize worker startup overhead by using fewer workers and\n    larger chunks for Phase 1 (which is the bottleneck).\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: 2-4)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for Phase 1\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        # Reduce worker count to minimize startup overhead\n        # Tokenization is the main bottleneck, balance with startup cost\n        num_workers = max(2, cpu_count() // 2)\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel Tokenization and TF Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    # This is the main CPU-bound work. Use larger chunks to reduce overhead.\n\n    # Ensure chunk size is large enough to justify worker startup\n    effective_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(documents) // (num_workers * 2))\n    doc_chunks = _chunks(documents, effective_chunk_size)\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_tokenize_documents_worker, doc_chunks)\n\n    # Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n\n    for chunk_result in chunk_results:\n        for doc_id, (term_set, tf_dict) in chunk_result.items():\n            doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf_dict\n            doc_terms[doc_id] = term_set\n            index.vocabulary.update(term_set)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Sequential Document Frequency Calculation\n    # ========================================================================\n    # This is sequential - no benefit to parallelization\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Compute IDF Scores (Sequential, minimal computation)\n    # ========================================================================\n\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Parallel Inverted Index and Document Vector Building\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Combine these into one worker pool to amortize startup cost\n\n    # Split into 2 tasks: one for inverted index, one for doc vectors\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    doc_ids_list = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n\n    worker_args = [\n        (\"inverted_index\", vocab_list, doc_term_freqs, index.idf),\n        (\"doc_vectors\", doc_ids_list, doc_term_freqs, index.idf),\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=2) as pool:\n        phase2_results = pool.map(_phase2_builder, worker_args)\n\n    # Extract results\n    for result_type, result_data in phase2_results:\n        if result_type == \"inverted_index\":\n            index.inverted_index.update(result_data)\n        elif result_type == \"doc_vectors\":\n            for doc_id, (doc_vector, norm) in result_data.items():\n                index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n                index.doc_norms[doc_id] = norm\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_worker(args: tuple) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to perform a single query search.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, documents_dict = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = documents_dict.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# Global state for worker processes - eliminates re-pickling\n_WORKER_INDEX = None\n_WORKER_DOC_TITLES = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index, doc_titles):\n    \"\"\"Initialize global state in worker process (called once per worker).\"\"\"\n    global _WORKER_INDEX, _WORKER_DOC_TITLES\n    _WORKER_INDEX = index\n    _WORKER_DOC_TITLES = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _batch_search_worker(args: tuple) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to process a batch of queries using global index.\n\n    Batching multiple queries per worker amortizes worker startup cost.\n    Global state avoids re-pickling the large index for each batch.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (queries_batch, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists, one per query\n    \"\"\"\n    queries_batch, top_k = args\n    all_results = []\n\n    for query in queries_batch:\n        # Use global _WORKER_INDEX instead of pickling it each time\n        result = _search_worker((query, _WORKER_INDEX, top_k, _WORKER_DOC_TITLES))\n        all_results.append(result)\n\n    return all_results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using batched processing.\n\n    Batches queries together to amortize worker process startup cost.\n    This is critical for good performance with moderate query counts.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: 4)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results list, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    if not queries:\n        return ([], 0.0)\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = 4  # Default to 4 workers for search\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Create document title lookup dictionary\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        for doc in documents:\n            doc_titles[doc.doc_id] = doc.title\n\n    # Batch queries to reduce worker startup overhead\n    # Each worker processes multiple queries\n    num_batches = min(num_workers, max(1, len(queries)))\n    query_batches = np.array_split(np.array(queries), num_batches)\n\n    # Prepare arguments for workers (one batch per worker)\n    worker_args = [\n        (list(batch), index, top_k, doc_titles)\n        for batch in query_batches\n        if len(batch) > 0\n    ]\n\n    # Execute parallel search with batched queries\n    with Pool(processes=len(worker_args)) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_batch_search_worker, worker_args)\n\n    # Flatten results: merge batch_results into a single list\n    all_results = [item for batch in batch_results for item in batch]\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return (all_results, elapsed)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=None, help=\"Number of workers\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--chunk-size\", type=int, default=500, help=\"Chunk size for parallel processing\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Build index\n    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index (parallel)...\")\n    result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n        documents,\n        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n        chunk_size=args.chunk_size,\n    )\n\n    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n\n    # Perform search\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for: '{args.query}'\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\n    all_results, search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        [args.query],\n        result.index,\n        args.top_k,\n        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n        documents=documents,\n    )\n\n    results = all_results[0] if all_results else []\n\n    print(f\"Search completed in {search_time*1000:.2f} ms\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop {len(results)} results:\")\n    for i, res in enumerate(results, 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}. [{res.score:.4f}] {res.title} (doc_id: {res.doc_id})\")\n\n    return result\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":594,"oldLines":6,"newStart":594,"newLines":7,"lines":["     Search for multiple queries in parallel using batched processing."," ","     Batches queries together to amortize worker process startup cost.","+    Uses worker process global state to avoid re-pickling the large index.","     This is critical for good performance with moderate query counts."," ","     Args:"]},{"oldStart":625,"oldLines":15,"newStart":626,"newLines":20,"lines":["     num_batches = min(num_workers, max(1, len(queries)))","     query_batches = np.array_split(np.array(queries), num_batches)"," ","-    # Prepare arguments for workers (one batch per worker)","+    # Prepare arguments for workers (only queries and top_k, index goes via initializer)","     worker_args = [","-        (list(batch), index, top_k, doc_titles)","+        (list(batch), top_k)","         for batch in query_batches","         if len(batch) > 0","     ]"," ","-    # Execute parallel search with batched queries","-    with Pool(processes=len(worker_args)) as pool:","+    # Execute parallel search with worker initialization","+    # Pool.map with initializer: loads index once per worker process, not per batch","+    with Pool(","+        processes=len(worker_args),","+        initializer=_init_search_worker,","+        initargs=(index, doc_titles),","+    ) as pool:","         batch_results = pool.map(_batch_search_worker, worker_args)"," ","     # Flatten results: merge batch_results into a single list"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

to their normalized frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TF-IDF Index Data Structures\n# idf_dict[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            result[doc_id] = (doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared))\n        return
